description: It all started with a simple idea: keep the taste but ditch the grease. That's why you won't find a single fryer in our restaurant. So get ready to take a trip to a place where everything is baked — not fried. That's why we say 'Get Baked' and enjoy the trip. No passport required! The first Island Wing Company restaurant opened in 2012. The concept was created by a pair of free-thinking restaurant entrepreneurs who happen to love the island life. Together, the husband and wife team had spent the prior 15 years building a highly successful 300 unit restaurant chain based in Destin, FL. Once again they had an idea for another unique restaurant concept. They paired that vision with an amazing team with decades of restaurant and franchise experience to create Island Wing Company. It's always a fun time on the Island. If you're looking for good food, delicious drinks, and a lively atmosphere to enjoy with friends and family – look no further. You don't need a passport to savor some of the best eats and drinks around. We've perfected baking, steaming, and grilling so you can find your favorite from our extensive menu including crispy chicken wings, juicy burgers and more. Choose the perfect drink from our large draft beer list, frozen drinks, and cocktails. Sit back and enjoy Island Time with us when you stop by to watch the latest game, enjoy live music, or catch up with loved ones. Come find out why it's always a little better on the Island.
